<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习在训练层面仍依赖经典优化循环——通过测量得到的梯度估计由经典外环更新参数，量子相干性在训练动力学中完全不发挥作用，本质上与经典训练无异。作者希望挖掘量子相干在训练过程中的潜在优势，实现真正全量子的训练机制。

Method: 将参数置于可叠加的量子寄存器中，构造以损失函数为"能量"的哈密顿量；系统在参数叠加态的均匀初态下进行幺正演化，通过量子干涉使概率幅向低损失参数构型聚集。使用块编码（block encodings）与哈密顿量模拟实现显式构造，可适用于任意参数化量子电路；批处理训练通过顺序哈密顿量演化实现。

Result: 在二分类任务和干涉相位估计两类数值实验中，演化后的分布峰值落在最优参数处，性能与基于梯度的训练方法相当。

Conclusion: 相干量子学习原理上与容错实现兼容，为量子机器学习提供了一种无需梯度计算与经典反馈的端到端量子训练范式，有潜力成为经典优化环路的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Coherent Quantum Learning (CQL), a training framework for quantum learning models in which the model parameters are quantum degrees of freedom evolved under a Hamiltonian that encodes the loss function. Current quantum machine learning retains classical optimization: parameters are updated by a classical outer loop using gradient estimates from measurements, and quantum coherence has no role in the training dynamics, just as in any classical treatment of the same problem. In the quantum case, a parameter register initialized in superposition evolves unitarily, and probability amplitude concentrates near low-loss configurations through interference, without gradient computation or classical feedback. We give an explicit construction using block encodings and Hamiltonian simulation, applicable to arbitrary parameterized circuits. Numerical experiments on binary classification and interferometric phase estimation confirm that the evolved distribution peaks at the optimal parameters, matching gradient-based performance. The construction is compatible in principle with fault-tolerant implementations and extends to batched training via sequential Hamiltonian evolution.

</details>


### [100] [Q-Edge: Symmetry-Reduced Quantum Simulation of Structured Extreme Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03706)
*Hongrui Zhang,Paolo Recchia,Ying Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Edge（量子极端依赖引擎），通过对称性将高维极端依赖的指数级状态空间无损压缩为紧凑的轨道空间表示，从而突破高维多元极值模拟的组合复杂性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多元极值模拟的主要瓶颈在于依赖结构的组合复杂性（指数级增长），而非极端观测的稀缺性。作者旨在通过利用对称性来克服这一根本性的计算挑战。

Method: 证明对称性可提供无损轨道空间表示，保留结构化极端依赖的同时将指数级依赖空间替换为紧凑的对称类集合。基于此原理开发Q-Edge量子框架，直接在轨道空间中操作，将对称性转移到数据表示层面而非量子电路中，使无约束量子生成模型能够利用大幅缩减的状态空间。

Result: 以30维问题为例，约160万角状态坍缩为256个轨道状态，所需量子表示从约21个量子比特减少至8个，实现了数量级的资源缩减。

Conclusion: Q-Edge建立了可扩展量子模拟结构化极端依赖的一般性计算原理，为高维极端系统仿真和数字孪生提供了有效路径。

Abstract: High-dimensional simulation of multivariate extremes is fundamentally limited by the combinatorial complexity of dependence, often more than by the scarcity of extreme observations. We show that symmetry admits a lossless orbit-space representation that preserves structured extreme dependence while replacing an exponentially large dependence space with a compact set of symmetry classes. Based on this principle, we develop Q-Edge (Quantum Extreme Dependence Engine), a symmetry-reduced quantum framework that operates directly in orbit space, enabling scalable simulation and digital twins of structured extreme systems. By transferring symmetry into the data representation rather than the quantum circuit, Q-Edge allows unconstrained quantum generative models to exploit dramatically reduced state spaces. For a 30-dimensional problem, approximately 1.6 million angular states collapse to 256 orbit states, reducing the required quantum representation from about 21 qubits to 8. Our results establish a general computational principle for scalable quantum simulation of structured extreme dependence.

</details>


### [101] [Analog quantum simulation of bosonic and anyonic models with flux-driven transmons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03737)
*Isak Lyngfelt,Jorge Fernández-Pendás,Göran Johansson,Laura Garciá-Álvarez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用电容耦合磁通可调transmon阵列模拟玻色-哈伯德模型和任意子-哈伯德模型的通用协议，通过交替调制频率共振驱动多体跃迁，实现最多每格点三个玻色子的相互作用和密度依赖跳跃振幅调控，并首次提出transmon上任意子-哈伯德模型的模拟方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统在经典计算机上难以模拟，量子模拟提供了一种替代方案，直接在可控量子硬件上实现目标系统的动力学。此前在超导量子硬件上模拟玻色-哈伯德和任意子-哈伯德模型仍缺乏通用且高效的协议。

Method: 使用电容耦合的磁通可调transmon晶格；通过交替模式调制transmon频率，共振驱动多个多体跃迁；调节在位相互作用和密度依赖跳跃振幅（最多支持每格点三个玻色子，总粒子数无限制）；向调制添加相位使跃迁振幅变为复值，从而模拟任意子统计相位。

Result: 对驱动transmon阵列的实验参数数值模拟表明，该方法能在多种相互作用强度和统计相位下复现目标模型的动力学特征，包括相互作用依赖的局域化和任意子量子行走中依赖统计的对称性破缺现象。

Conclusion: 该协议为在超导量子硬件上模拟玻色-哈伯德和任意子-哈伯德模型提供了切实可行的通用方案，且实验参数现实可行，能够捕捉目标量子多体物理的核心特征。

Abstract: When quantum particles interact, many-body phenomena that are hard to simulate classically emerge. Quantum analog simulation offers an alternative in which the target system's dynamics is directly realized in controllable quantum hardware. Here, we give a general protocol for simulating the Bose-Hubbard and anyon-Hubbard models using lattices of capacitively coupled flux-tunable transmons. By modulating the transmon frequencies in an alternating pattern, we resonantly drive multiple many-body transitions and can tune the on-site interaction and density-dependent hopping amplitudes for up to three bosons per site, with no additional restriction on the total particle number. By adding phases to the modulation, which renders the transition amplitudes complex-valued, we propose the first simulation protocol for the anyon-Hubbard model with transmons. Numerical simulations of the driven transmon arrays with experimentally realistic parameters reproduce the characteristic dynamics of the target models across a range of interaction strengths and statistical phases, including the interaction-dependent localization and the statistics-dependent asymmetry of the anyonic quantum walk.

</details>


### [102] [Thermodynamic Irreversibility from Inaccessible Endogenous Quantum Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03748)
*Borhan Ahmadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究微观幺正动力学中熵产生的机制，指出孤立有限系统的熵增并非由于微观信息丢失，而是隐藏信息未能组织起恢复宏观秩序的流。作者推导了精确熵率，将熵扩散与回返定向流分离，并揭示了哈密顿量与微观状态如何组织隐藏信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题：在微观幺正动力学完全保真保留所有信息的情况下，为何孤立有限系统仍表现出稳健的热力学熵增窗口？即为何隐藏在受限记录之外的信息通常无法重建低熵宏观态？

Method: 对固定投影记录，将每步有限演化精确分解为仅由记录预测的演化部分和由未解析微观结构承载的精确修正项。记录贡献仅从时间二阶开始，因此记录的全部瞬时变化源于宏观态间的隐藏流。作者推导出精确熵率，将熵扩散与回返定向流分离并将流分解为能隙振幅，相同间隙的跃迁相干叠加。分别在经典保测映射和量子混动动力学中进行对比验证，并设计了分布级检验区分隐藏动力学活动与有限步回返。

Result: 互作用混合动力学中流功率分布广泛；自由与刻意设计的一致性控制系统逐步将流功率集中，工程化动力学可重建低熵宏观态。不匹配间隙的跃迁相干叠加方式揭示了哈密顿量与微观态如何组织隐藏信息用于回返。分布级检验可分离隐藏动力学活动与有限步回返效应，经典对应部分表明构造的若干环节并非量子特有的。

Conclusion: 在给定记录与观测窗口内，不可逆性的本质不是微观信息的丢失，而是微观信息未能组织起能够恢复宏观秩序的流。

Abstract: Microscopic unitary dynamics preserves all fine information, yet an isolated finite system can show a robust thermodynamic window in which the entropy associated with a restricted record rises. We ask why information hidden from the current record usually fails to rebuild a low-entropy macrostate. For a fixed projective record, every finite step separates exactly into the evolution predicted from the record alone and an exact correction carried by unresolved microscopic structure. The record-only contribution starts only at second order in time, so all instantaneous change of the record comes from hidden currents between macrostates. We derive the exact entropy rate, separate entropy-spreading from return-oriented currents, and resolve those currents into energy-gap amplitudes. Transitions with the same gap add coherently, revealing how the Hamiltonian and the microscopic state organize hidden information for return. In interacting mixing dynamics the current power is spread over many frequencies; free and deliberately commensurate controls progressively concentrate it, and the engineered dynamics reconstructs a low-entropy macrostate. An independent distribution-level test separates hidden dynamical activity from finite-step return, and an exact classical measure-preserving counterpart shows which parts of the construction are not uniquely quantum. Within the specified record and observation window, irreversibility is therefore not loss of microscopic information, but the failure of that information to organize currents that restore macroscopic order.

</details>


### [103] [Metricity of separable quantum optimal transport with the Hilbert-Schmidt cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03769)
*Tomasz Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了与反对称子空间正交投影相关的可分量子最优输运成本的平方根在密度矩阵之间定义了真正的距离，等价地确立了二阶Beatty-França量子最优输运构造的三角不等式，并证明了相应可分SWAP保真度距离的度量性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优输运是量子信息论中的重要工具，但可分量子最优输运成本在密度矩阵上定义距离的问题（即三角不等式）尚未解决。由于标准的胶合（gluing）论证不适用于可分设定，需要新的数学工具来证明其度量性质。

Method: 用凸包对偶（convex-roof duality）替代不可用的胶合论证，并结合一个与维度无关的Hermitian算子插值结果，证明了平方根成本满足三角不等式，从而确立其作为密度矩阵之间真实距离的地位。

Result: 成功证明了平方根形式的可分量子最优输运成本（由反对称子空间投影诱导）构成密度矩阵之间的真实距离；等价地，建立了二阶Beatty-França构造的三角不等式，以及可分SWAP保真度对应距离的度量性。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了量子最优输运理论中可分成本度量性质的关键空白，表明即使没有经典胶合论证，通过凸包对偶和算子插值等新工具仍可建立三角不等式，为量子态空间上的几何结构提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that the square root of the separable quantum optimal transport cost associated with the orthogonal projection onto the antisymmetric subspace defines a genuine distance between density matrices. Equivalently, this establishes the triangle inequality for the order-two Beatty-França quantum optimal transport construction induced by the Hilbert-Schmidt distance between pure states. The result also proves metricity of the corresponding distance derived from separable SWAP fidelity. The proof replaces the unavailable gluing argument by convex-roof duality and a dimension-independent interpolation result for Hermitian operators.

</details>


### [104] [Quantisation of Abstract Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03778)
*Mingsheng Ying,Zhicheng Zhang,Kean Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在泛代数框架内引入抽象量子数据类型的概念，为量子编程中的数据抽象提供代数基础，并展示经典数据类型的量化构造及其等式规范的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编程中需要一种形式化、代数化的数据抽象方法来描述和处理经典数据类型的量子化，而现有的数据抽象理论主要针对经典计算环境。

Method: 在泛代数框架下定义抽象量子数据类型，形式化经典数据类型的量化过程，证明等式规范可以健全地提升到量子设置中。比特预言机和相位预言机作为该构想的特例，并通过量子数组和量子纠错码验证框架的适用性。

Result: 经典数据类型的量化过程被严格形式化，两种标准的量化方法（比特预言机和相位预言机）被统一为该一般构想的特例。该框架成功应用于量子数组和量子纠错码，并建立了量化过程保持经典数据类型结构性关系（嵌入、同构、积）的条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子编程中的数据类型抽象提供了泛代数基础，表明经典数据类型的代数规范可以系统性地、可验证地迁移到量子计算环境中。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a notion of abstract quantum data type within the framework of universal algebra. This notion provides an algebraic foundation for describing data abstraction in quantum programming. We formally define a quantisation of classical data types and show that their equational specifications can be soundly lifted to the quantum setting. Two standard quantisation methods for classical functions, namely the bit oracle and the phase oracle, arise as special cases of this general construction. We illustrate the framework with applications to quantum arrays and quantum error-correcting codes, showing how they can be understood through the lens of data-type quantisation. We further establish conditions under which quantisation preserves structural relationships and constructions of classical data types, including embeddings, isomorphisms, and products.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum Query Complexity of Finding a Tarski Fixed Point on a High-Dimensional Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03802)
*Tongyang Li,Weiran Ma,Ziyi Yang,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子计算模型中寻找单调函数Tarski不动点的查询复杂度问题，证明了在[n]^k上寻找单调函数不动点需要Ω(k log n)次量子查询下界，并提出了新的树-过滤对抗方法（Tree-Filtration Adversary Method）来构造对抗矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tarski不动点定理在经济学、博弈论和编程语言等领域有广泛应用，其算法变体的查询复杂度在经典模型中已被广泛研究，但在量子设置中的研究相对较少，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用非负谱对抗方法（nonnegative spectral adversary method）证明量子查询下界，为此开发了树-过滤对抗方法（Tree-Filtration Adversary Method）来构造对抗矩阵，该方法为非负谱对抗方法提供了更透明的组合解释。

Result: 证明了在[n]^k上寻找单调函数不动点的Ω(k log n)量子查询下界。在n=2和k=1两个极端情形下，该下界分别匹配经典的Ω(k)和Ω(log n)下界。当n,k≥2时，若n<k，该下界优于最佳经典下界；若n≥k，与已知经典下界的差距在log n/log k因子以内。

Conclusion: 量子计算为Tarski不动点问题提供了比经典计算更紧的下界保证，同时新提出的树-过滤对抗方法不仅适用于本问题，还为处理具有树状结构和经典决策树下界直觉的困难实例提供了建立量子复杂度下界的通用方法论。

Abstract: The Knaster-Tarski fixed-point theorem states that every monotone function over a complete lattice has a fixed point. Beyond its fundamental role in order theory, the theorem and its algorithmic variants have found broad applications in areas such as economics, game theory, and programming languages. While the query complexity of finding a Tarski fixed point has been extensively studied in classical models, comparatively little is known in the quantum setting.
  We prove an $Ω(k\log n)$ quantum query lower bound for finding a fixed point of a monotone function on $[n]^k$, using the nonnegative spectral adversary method. In the two extremal regimes $n = 2$ and $k = 1$, our quantum lower bound matches the previous classical lower bounds $Ω(k)$ and $Ω(\log n)$, respectively. For $n, k\geq 2$, our bound improves the best previous classical lower bound when $n < k$ and is within a factor of $\log n / \log k$ compared to the known classical lower bound when $n \geq k$. To construct the adversary matrix, we develop the Tree--Filtration Adversary Method. Besides yielding our lower bound, the method offers a more transparent combinatorial interpretation of the nonnegative spectral adversary method. When the hard instances of a problem admit a tree-like organization and suggest an intuition analogous to classical decision-tree lower bounds, our method provide a promising approach to establishing quantum complexity lower bounds.

</details>


### [106] [Energetic Costs of Subspace Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03825)
*Jakub Czartowski,Felix C. Binder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从热力学角度系统表征了子空间量子纠错码中维持可复用综合征寄存器所需的最小功。基于Knill-Laflamme条件构建有效综合征态，其von Neumann熵为可复用综合征存储的理想功提供下界；进一步针对稳定子码在独立局域Pauli噪声下的两种综合征表示层次（抽象错误标签层与奇偶校验层），量化了多样性、校验权重和位级开销对熵成本的贡献，并在五量子比特码、Steane码、广义Shor码和旋转表面码上予以实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错作为"熵泵"将噪声引起的量子不确定性转化为辅助存储中的综合征信息，但重复运行需要清除这些存储，这不可避免地为纠错过程带来能量开销。此前研究缺乏对这一热力学代价的系统性刻画，尤其是其如何依赖于码、噪声与综合征信息表示的联合结构尚不清楚，理解并最小化该代价对容错量子计算的能效至关重要。

Method: (1) 从Knill-Laflamme条件出发构造有效综合征态，其von Neumann熵作为可复用综合征寄存器所需理想功的理论下界；(2) 通过测量效率参数量化投影测量读出所引入的额外熵差；(3) 对稳定子码在独立局域Pauli噪声下分析两种综合征表示层次——抽象错误标签层面（单量子比特错误简并可降低前导熵）与奇偶校验层面（低权重校验在低噪区降低边际熵）；(4) 界定因保留并单独擦除各测量结果而产生的"位级低效率"；(5) 在五量子比特码、Steane码、广义Shor码和旋转表面码上分别计算验证。

Result: 论文建立了子空间QECC综合征存储能量代价的层级关系：有效综合征态的von Neumann熵给出了理想功下界；投影读出导致的熵增可通过测量低效率定量刻画；单比特错误简并（退化码）在抽象标签层降低前导熵；低权重校验在低噪声区降低奇偶校验层面的边际熵；位级存储与单独擦除产生额外热力学开销。多种码型上的数值结果验证了码结构、噪声与测量方案共同决定理想热力学负担。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了控制子空间量子纠错理想热力学负担的码、噪声和测量结构因素，建立了综合征记忆能量代价的层级，为设计能量高效的容错量子计算提供理论基础和设计准则。

Abstract: Quantum error correction acts as an entropy pump, transferring noise-induced uncertainty from a protected quantum system into syndrome information stored in an auxiliary memory. Repeated operation requires this memory to be cleared which unavoidably contributes to the energetic cost of error correction. Here, we characterise this contribution for subspace quantum error-correcting codes and identify how it depends on the joint structure of the code, the noise, and the representation of the retained syndrome information. Starting from the Knill-Laflamme conditions, we construct an effective syndrome state whose von Neumann entropy sets a lower bound on the ideal work required to maintain a reusable syndrome register. Projective syndrome readout generally generates additional entropy, and we quantify the resulting gap through measurement inefficiency. We then specialise to stabiliser codes under independent local Pauli noise and analyse two classical levels of syndrome representation. At the level of abstract error labels, degeneracies among single-qubit errors reduce the leading-order entropy of processed recovery labels. At the parity-check level, lower-weight checks reduce the marginal entropy generated by individual measurement outcomes in the low-noise regime. We identify the additional burden associated with retaining and separately erasing these outcomes as a bit-level inefficiency, and illustrate both costs for the five-qubit, Steane, generalised Shor, and rotated surface codes. Our results establish a hierarchy of syndrome-memory energetic costs and identify the code, noise, and measurement structures that control the ideal thermodynamic burden of subspace quantum error correction.

</details>


### [107] [Exact Compatibility Geometry of Three-Qubit Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03864)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三量子比特纯态体系中成对并发度与三重纠缠度量（three-tangle）之间的置换对称支撑关系，并通过引入四维相容性体概念，将所提出的全局关系与经典的CKW不等式统一视为同一可达集合的不同支撑方向，给出了三量子比特纯态完整四维相容性的充要半代数刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子纠缠的多体系统中，如何统一地刻画出不同纠缠分量（如两体并发度与三体tangle）之间的约束关系是一个核心问题。已有的CKW关系是一种经典的不等式约束，但作者旨在寻找更具对称性的全局关系，并建立更一般的几何框架以统一理解各类纠缠相容性约束。

Method: 1) 构造三量子比特体系中并发度对与three-tangle之间的置换对称支撑关系，其极值仅在对称的W态和GHZ局域幺正等价类中取得。2) 引入四维相容性体概念，将全局关系和CKW关系视为同一可达集合的不同支撑方向。3) 利用半代数几何方法给出三量子比特纯态完全四维相容性的充要刻画。4) 将该框架推广至任意并发度生成的纠缠度量。

Result: 获得了置换对称的三体纠缠支撑关系，其极值仅由W类与GHZ类态取得；通过四维相容性体统一了新的全局关系与CKW关系；基于此给出了缺失两体纠缠分量在其余分量已知时所能取值的精确无参数区间描述；该构造对任意并发度导生的纠缠测度同样适用。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一个统一的几何框架，能够将不同类型的纠缠相容性约束（如CKW不等式与提出的新对称关系）放置于同一可达集合的视角下理解，并为三量子比特纯态提供了完整的维度双向纠缠相容性半代数刻画，且该方法具有向一般并发度量推广的普适性。

Abstract: We present a permutation-symmetric supporting relation between the pairwise concurrences and the three-tangle in three-qubit system, whose extrema are attained only by the symmetric $W$ and GHZ local-unitary classes. Furthermore, by introducing the concept of a four-dimensional compatibility body, we show that our proposed global relation and the previous CKW relation can be uniformly viewed as different supporting directions of the same achievable set. Subsequently, we provide a necessary-and-sufficient semi-algebraic characterization of the complete four-dimensional compatibility of a three-qubit pure state. Based on this, we can conversely describe the compatibility between entangled components: given the remaining entangled components, the missing two-body entangled component can only take values within a precise parameter-free interval. We demonstrate that the above construction also applies to arbitrary concurrence-generated entanglement measures.

</details>


### [108] [Ultra-Precise Quantum Projective Designs in Constant Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03925)
*Qingyue Zhang,Junjie Chen,Zhou You,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稀疏可交换（commuting）电路系综，由稀疏Clifford相位层与独立单比特Clifford门组成，可在相对误差意义下复现2阶和3阶Haar矩，构成ε-近似射影设计。该构造在ancilla-free情况下可实现O(log(n/ε))的量子深度，也可用O(n log(n/ε))个辅助比特实现常数深度（深度7）的适应性实现，且对数级交互度在该电路族内渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的Haar随机性代价高昂且通常并非必需，而现有的浅层量子设计方案依赖特定范式。作者旨在利用可交换相位电路的固有矩结构，以极低的量子资源（稀疏交互、浅深度）在严格的相对误差意义下再现Haar低阶矩，从而弥合精确Haar随机性与实用稀疏动力学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 构造一个由稀疏Clifford相位层加上独立单比特Clifford门的电路系综。分析中利用可交换相位电路（commuting phase circuits）的内禀矩结构：在二阶通过已有的矩分析，在三阶则需要引入新的块分解（block decomposition）与组合分析，以证明该系综形成ε-近似的射影2-和3-设计（相对误差意义）。同时给出两种实现方案：无辅助比特的全连接架构O(log(n/ε))深度实现，以及用O(n log(n/ε))个辅助比特实现自适应深度7的常数深度实现。

Result: 该电路系综在相对误差意义下构成ε-近似射影2-和3-设计；所需的对数级交互度在此电路族内渐近最优。实现了O(log(n/ε))的量子深度（无辅助）以及深度为7的常数深度适应性实现（需O(n log(n/ε))个辅助比特）。三阶分析中提出的新块分解方法也为迈向更高阶浅层设计提供了方向。

Conclusion: 精确的Haar类统计可由稀疏可交换动力学以极低的量子资源涌现，该结果对随机化表征、量子计量、量子算法和多体物理等应用具有广泛意义，并为发展更高阶的浅层量子设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Random quantum objects are powerful resources for quantum information processing, yet exact Haar randomness is costly and typically unnecessary. We introduce an explicit sparse commuting circuit ensemble on $n$ qubits that reproduces low-order Haar moments in the stringent relative-error sense. The circuit consists of a sparse Clifford phase layer followed by independent single-qubit Clifford gates. Acting on a simple product state, the resulting ensemble forms $ε$-approximate projective $2$- and $3$-designs in relative error, with the required logarithmic interaction degree being asymptotically optimal within this circuit family. It admits an ancilla-free implementation of quantum depth $O(\log(n/ε))$ on an all-to-all architecture, as well as an adaptive constant-depth implementation---in fact, depth seven---using $O(n\log(n/ε))$ ancilla qubits. Departing from existing shallow-design paradigms, our analysis exploits the intrinsic moment structure of commuting phase circuits; at third order, this requires a new block decomposition and combinatorial analysis that also suggests a route toward higher-order shallow designs. Our results show that precise Haar-like statistics can emerge from sparse commuting dynamics with remarkably low quantum resources, with applications to randomized characterization, quantum metrology, quantum algorithms, and many-body physics.

</details>


### [109] [Approximate maximum-likelihood decoding via truncated free energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03928)
*Yuanqi Liu,Weilei Zeng,Junjie Wu,Lingling Lao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出AMLD（近似最大似然解码）框架，通过回收随机内部解码器丢弃的候选样本，构建逐类截断自由能估计器来近似最大似然解码。实验表明AMLD在多种码型和噪声下缩小了MWD与MLD之间的阈值差距，最高可将逻辑错误率降低13%，并具有线性经典开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然解码（MLD）虽然能实现稳定子码的最小逻辑错误率，但其精确评估是#P-hard的。实际流水线采用最小权重解码（MWD）进行近似，但MWD只保留每个综合征的最低权重恢复，丢弃了陪集简并性（coset degeneracy）这一重要信息，导致性能与MLD存在差距。

Method: AMLD是一个黑盒框架，它回收随机内部解码器产生的候选样本，为每个逻辑类构建一个逐类截断自由能（truncated free-energy）估计器。该估计器被证明下界为精确自由能、上界为经验最小权重。AMLD返回最小化估计自由能的逻辑类，仅需线性经典开销。该框架在SA-based Ising解码器和BP-OSD候选池上均可应用，无需匹配枚举或码特定的张量网络收缩。

Result: 在SA-based Ising解码基准上，AMLD在toric码和color码的bit-flip与depolarizing噪声下，最多闭合了MWD-MLD阈值差距的83%。最大阈值改进出现在6.6.6 color码depolarizing噪声下，从17.28%提升到18.62%。在[[144,12,12]]双二元环码（bivariate-bicycle）上，p=0.05时AMLD相对MWD（基于相同BP-OSD候选池）将逻辑错误率降低了13%。

Conclusion: AMLD通过利用随机解码器已生成的候选样本和陪集简并信息，以线性额外开销实现了接近MLD的解码性能，是一种通用且高效的黑盒近似最大似然解码方案，适用于包括双二元环码在内的多种码型。

Abstract: Maximum-likelihood decoding (MLD) achieves the minimum logical error rate of stabilizer codes under known i.i.d. Pauli noise, but its exact evaluation is \#P-hard. Practical pipelines therefore approximate MLD by minimum-weight decoding (MWD), retaining only the lowest-weight recovery per syndrome and discarding the coset degeneracy. The minimum-weight search is in turn implemented by stochastic solvers. We introduce approximate maximum-likelihood decoding (AMLD), a black-box framework that recycles the candidate samples discarded by stochastic inner decoders into a per-class truncated free-energy estimator. For every logical class represented in the candidate pool, the estimator is provably bounded below by the exact free energy and above by the empirical minimum weight. AMLD returns the logical class minimizing the estimated free energy with linear classical overhead. In SA-based Ising-decoder benchmarks, AMLD closes up to $83\%$ of the MWD--MLD threshold gap across the toric and color codes under bit-flip and depolarizing noise. The largest threshold improvement, from $17.28\%$ to $18.62\%$, occurs on the $6.6.6$ color code under depolarizing noise. We further demonstrate AMLD on the $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate-bicycle code, whose bit-flip decoding problem has a hypergraph structure. This application requires neither matching-based enumeration nor code-specific tensor-network contraction. At $p=0.05$, AMLD reduces the logical error rate by $13\%$ relative to MWD evaluated on the same BP-OSD candidate pool.

</details>


### [110] [On the geometry and typicality of quantum magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03944)
*Zhenhuan Liu,Z-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了n量子比特系统中满足Tr(ρ²)≤1/(d-a_*)（a_*=0.458327…）的所有量子态位于stabilizer多胞体内部，即无magic。结合高维多胞体的几何性质，定量刻画了stabilizer多胞体的Hilbert-Schmidt内半径和体积半径，揭示了随机诱导态中magic的典型性，并证明了magic出现的尖锐相变以及stabilizer多胞体面数的指数级上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化stabilizer多胞体的几何结构对于理解量子计算中magic资源理论至关重要，此前对stabilizer多胞体的几何复杂度（如体积、面数）缺乏精确的定量刻画，且随机态中magic的典型行为（相变点位置）尚未被严格确定。

Method: 通过证明满足Tr(ρ²)≤1/(d-a_*)的态全部位于stabilizer多胞体内，结合高维凸多胞体的通用几何性质（内半径、体积半径的关系），推导stabilizer多胞体的几何参数；利用随机诱导态的分布特性分析magic概率的相变，并通过组合几何方法给出面数的指数级上下界。

Result: (1) 建立了Hilbert-Schmidt内半径和体积半径的定量估计；(2) 给出随机诱导态具有magic的概率尖锐相变，相变维度k_*介于Ω(d²/log²d)与O(d²)之间；(3) stabilizer多胞体的面数介于exp[Ω(d²/log²d)]与exp[O(d²log²d)]之间，大幅改进此前拟多项式下界，说明精确描述无magic区域需要量子比特数上的双重指数级线性不等式数目。

Conclusion: 结果表明stabilizer多胞体在给定顶点数情况下展现出接近高维多胞体可允许的最大几何复杂度，揭示了magic资源在量子态空间中随维度增长变得高度典型，同时精确描述无magic区域在计算上代价极高。

Abstract: We prove that, for an $n$-qubit system of dimension $d=2^n$, every state satisfying $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^2)\le 1/(d-a_\ast)$, with $a_\ast=0.458327\cdots$, lies inside the stabilizer polytope and is therefore magic-free. Combining this result with general geometric properties of high-dimensional polytopes, we establish quantitative estimates for the Hilbert--Schmidt inradius and volume radius of the stabilizer polytope, and use them to characterize the typicality of magic in random induced states obtained by tracing out a $k$-dimensional subsystem from a $d\times k$-dimensional Haar-random pure state. We prove a sharp phase transition in the probability of such states having magic, whose transition dimension $k_\star$ is bounded between $Ω(d^2/\log^2d)$ and $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$. We further prove that the number of facets of the stabilizer polytope lies between $\exp[Ω(d^2/\log^2 d)]$ and $\exp[\mathcal{O}(d^2\log^2 d)]$, substantially improving upon the previous quasipolynomial lower bound and implying that any exact description of the magic-free region requires a doubly exponential number of linear inequalities in the number of qubits. Overall, our results show that the stabilizer polytope exhibits near-maximal geometric complexity allowed for a high-dimensional polytope with a certain number of vertices.

</details>


### [111] [Robust multi-hypothesis quantum-state discrimination under unknown common unitary perturbations via least favorable priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04020)
*Daichi Fujiki,Fuyuhiko Tanaka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了所有候选量子态受同一未知幺正扰动影响时的鲁棒K元量子态判别问题。将问题建模为关于扰动的极小极大决策问题，提出使用与最小有利先验（LFP）相关联的贝叶斯最优集体测量的方法。对于有限离散化的扰动空间，证明LFP可通过半定规划计算，且对应值等于有限网格极小极大成功概率。数值实验（二元非正交量子比特模型和三元非正交量子三态模型）表明LFP测量能显著平坦化成功概率剖面并提升最坏情况成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际量子态判别中，所有候选态可能共同受到一个未知的公共幺正扰动影响。该扰动不是待识别的标签，而是一个妨碍因子，会降低固定测量的性能。需要设计一种对这类未知扰动具有鲁棒性的判别测量方案。

Method: 将鲁棒判别问题形式化为在可能扰动空间上的极小极大决策问题；提出利用最小有利先验（LFP）对应的贝叶斯最优集体测量作为鲁棒策略；对有限离散化空间，证明LFP可通过半定规划求解，且该方案的极小极大成功概率与有限网格上的极小极大值一致。

Result: 在二元非正交量子比特模型与非正交三态qutrit模型的数值演示中，LFP测量显著平坦化了成功概率剖面，相对于参考点最优测量和均匀先验贝叶斯测量，改善了最坏情况下的成功概率。LFP将权重集中于扰动空间中主动限制鲁棒判别性能的区域。

Conclusion: LFP方法既提供了构造性的鲁棒测量设计方案，又提供了对困难扰动参数区域的诊断性描述，表明将权重集中在限制性能的扰动区域是实现鲁棒K元量子态判别的高效策略。

Abstract: We study robust K-ary quantum-state discrimination when all candidate states are affected by the same unknown common unitary perturbation. The unknown perturbation does not represent the label to be identified, but acts as a nuisance factor that changes the performance of a fixed measurement. We formulate the problem as a minimax decision problem over the possible perturbations and propose using the Bayes-optimal collective measurement associated with a least favorable prior (LFP) on the nuisance-parameter space. For a finite discretization of this space, we show that the LFP can be computed by a semidefinite program and that the corresponding value coincides with the finite-grid minimax success probability. As numerical demonstrations, we consider a binary nonorthogonal qubit model and a nonorthogonal three-state qutrit model with an unknown common unitary perturbation. The LFP-based measurement substantially flattens the success-probability profile and improves the worst-case success probability compared with a reference-point optimal measurement and a uniform-prior Bayes measurement. The resulting LFP concentrates its weight on regions of the nuisance-parameter space that actively limit the robust discrimination performance, thereby providing both a constructive measurement design and a diagnostic description of the difficult nuisance-parameter regimes.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum-Enhanced Phase Estimation with Photon-Added Even and Odd Coherent States in an SU(1,1) Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04064)
*Abdelmajid El Maaroufi,Mouad Ait Maskour,Bouchra Maroufi,Mohammed Daoud,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在SU(1,1)干涉仪中，利用m光子添加的偶/奇相干态作为非经典输入资源进行相位估计。通过强度探测和误差传播法评估相位灵敏度，并利用量子Fisher信息和量子Cramér-Rao界确定极限精度。结果表明，光子添加显著增强相位灵敏度、提高量子Fisher信息并降低量子Cramér-Rao界，超越标准量子极限并逐渐逼近海森堡标度。偶相干态表现出微弱优势，但随着m增大，该优势逐渐减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过光子添加技术增强SU(1,1)干涉仪的相位估计能力，超越标准量子极限并接近海森堡极限，同时厘清输入态奇偶性对干涉度量性能的影响。

Method: 采用m光子添加的偶/奇相干态作为SU(1,1)干涉仪的非经典输入态；使用强度探测结合误差传播法计算相位灵敏度；基于量子Fisher信息计算量子Cramér-Rao界，给出测量的极限精度。

Result: 光子添加显著提高相位灵敏度和量子Fisher信息，降低量子Cramér-Rao界；灵敏度超过标准量子极限并随光子添加数增加逐步趋近海森堡标度；m光子添加的偶相干态比奇相干态具有微弱优势，但该优势随m增大而减弱。

Conclusion: 光子添加是提升SU(1,1)干涉仪相位估计精度的有效手段，且随着光子添加数增加，输入态初始奇偶性对干涉性能的影响逐渐被弱化，表明光子添加在决定干涉度量性能中占据主导地位。

Abstract: We investigate phase estimation in an SU(1,1) interferometer employing $m$-photon-added even and odd coherent states as nonclassical input resources. The phase sensitivity is evaluated through intensity detection and the error propagation method, while the ultimate precision limit is determined from the quantum Cramér-Rao bound with the quantum Fisher information serving as the relevant metrological quantity. Our results demonstrate that photon addition significantly enhances the phase sensitivity, increases the quantum Fisher information, and reduces the quantum Cramér-Rao bound, leading to a clear improvement over the corresponding even and odd coherent states. Furthermore, the achievable sensitivity exceeds the standard quantum limit and gradually approaches the Heisenberg scaling with increasing photon-addition number. We also find that the $m$-photon-added even coherent states exhibit a modest advantage over their odd counterparts. As $m$ increases, however, this distinction becomes progressively weaker, suggesting that photon addition diminishes the role of the initial parity of the coherent state in determining the interferometric performance.

</details>


### [113] [Small-quench Loschmidt dynamics near quantum critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04065)
*Kohei Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子临界点附近小突发淬火后的短时Loschmidt动力学，证明了Loschmidt率函数的初始二次增长由淬火算符每系统尺寸的方差控制。对于局域淬火算符，该方差密度恰好等于初态中相等的连接两点关联函数的空间求和，从而将早期Loschmidt响应与静态临界关联联系起来。基于临界处的幂律关联假设，作者按淬火算符的标度维度对短时系数的有限尺寸标度进行了分类，并在横向场Ising链中验证了不同淬火算符的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子相变临界点附近非平衡量子动力学，特别是Loschmidt回声动力学内部时间尺度的行为。作者希望建立早期Loschmidt动力学与静态临界关联之间的定量联系，从而通过短时动力学响应来探测量子临界现象。

Method: 通过解析推导，将小突发淬火后Loschmidt率函数的初二次增长率系数与淬火算符的方差密度联系起来；对局域算符，该方差密度等于等时连接两点关联函数求和。利用幂律关联假设和标度维度分析，对有限尺寸标度行为进行分类，并在横向场Ising链模型中分别用横向场和纵向场淬火加以验证；此外用后淬火哈密顿量的四阶累积量讨论超越二次区域的首次修正。

Result: 揭示了Loschmidt早期动力学与静态量子临界关联的直接解析关系；在临界幂律关联假设下，按淬火算符标度维度对短时系数进行了有限尺寸标度分类。在一维系统中，系数可以是有限的、对数增强的或代数增强的。横向场Ising链验证了不同淬火算符（横向场与纵向场淬火）对应不同临界算符时的行为差异。

Conclusion: 早期Loschmidt速率函数可以作为一种探测量子临界关联的动力学探针，其时间标度分类完全由淬火算符的临界标度维度决定。该框架为从短时动力学响应中提取静态临界信息提供了统一的理论描述，并通过四阶累积量扩展了对Loschmidt动力学超越二次近似的理解。

Abstract: We study the short-time Loschmidt dynamics after a small sudden quench near a quantum critical point. We show that the initial quadratic growth of the Loschmidt rate function is governed by the variance of the quench operator per system size. For a local quench operator, this variance density is exactly equal to the spatial sum of the equal-time connected two-point correlation function in the initial ground state. This relation connects the early-time Loschmidt response to static critical correlations. Assuming power-law correlations at criticality, we classify the finite-size scaling of the short-time coefficient by the scaling dimension of the quench operator. In one dimension, the coefficient is finite, logarithmically enhanced, or algebraically enhanced with system size. We illustrate this operator dependence in the transverse-field Ising chain, where transverse-field and longitudinal-field quenches couple to different critical operators. We also discuss the first correction beyond the quadratic regime using the fourth cumulant of the post-quench Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [114] [Algebraic Operator Decomposition: A Partitioned Architecture for Noise-Resilient Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04076)
*Wladimir Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种算子分解（AOD）架构，通过代数预分解将全局算子数学映射为可独立执行的局部算子，从而降低量子电路最大深度，但以经典重建和采样开销为代价。基于幺半群（Monoid）和 MapReduce 编程模型，利用可交换幺半群的特性消除"负概率税"和相位冲突，并定义适用于内积、级数展开、迹和卷积等线性算子的向量空间框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路深度是当前量子计算的关键瓶颈，直接影响量子错误累积和纠错可行性。现有量子纠错和错误缓解方法主要在量子执行后处理错误，缺乏在执行前进行代数层面的结构优化手段。

Method: 提出算子分解架构（AOD）：(1) 将全局算子代数预分解为可独立执行的局部算子，降低最大电路深度；(2) 借用计算机科学中幺半群（Monoid）概念——包含结合性组合函数和安全单位元的数据类型设计模式；(3) 定义 MapReduce 编程模型，以加法(+)作为归约器，利用自然稳定的可交换幺半群（commutative monoid）避免负概率问题和相位冲突；(4) 定义加法阿贝尔群上的线性算子向量空间，涵盖内积、级数展开、迹和卷积等算子类别。

Result: 论文提供了该范式的数学基础与仿真结果，展示AOD能够作为量子纠错和错误缓解的补充手段，通过在量子执行前进行代数分解来降低电路深度。

Conclusion: AOD 为量子电路深度优化提供了一种新的"执行前代数分解"路径，通过幺半群和Monoid代数结构实现算子分解，以经典重建与采样开销换取量子电路深度的降低，可作为现有量子纠错与错误缓解方法的有效补充。

Abstract: We present an operator-decomposition architecture that mathematically maps a global operator into independently executable local operators, reducing the maximum quantum circuit depth at the cost of classical reconstruction and sampling overhead. By framing complex Quantum Circuits around an operator in a vector space that can be algebraically pre-decomposed, AOD complements quantum error correction and error-mitigation approaches by performing algebraic decomposition before quantum execution.
  Our approach leans in the computer science definition of a Monoid: a design pattern and mathematical concept consisting of a data type, a combining function that is associative, and a safe identity (neutral) element that does not change other values when combined. Simulation wise we define a MapReduce programming model where the addition (+) is the reducer, thus leveraging a naturally stable commutative monoid which carries zero "negative-probability tax" or phase conflicts.
  Furthermore, we define a Vector Space of Linear Operators over Additive Abelian Groups that benefit from this paradigm, including: Inner Products, Series expansions, Traces and Convolutions. Finally, we present the mathematical foundations and simulation results for this paradigm.

</details>


### [115] [Frequency-Multiplexed Parallel Gates for Quantum LDPC Codes in a Two-Dimensional Ion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04081)
*G. -X. Tang,L. -M. Duan,Y. -K. Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在二维离子晶体上通过频率复用实现并行非局域纠缠门，用于量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码的综合征测量，避免物理移动离子带来的速度瓶颈。以[[248,10,18]]二元循环码和512离子晶体为例，在现实噪声参数下可实现10^-12的逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码具有高编码率，但综合征测量需要非局域纠缠门。现有方案通过物理移动离子来实现非局域耦合，但随着离子数增加移动速度变慢，成为性能瓶颈。因此需要一种无需物理移动就能实现并行非局域门的方法。

Method: 在二维离子晶体上利用频率复用（frequency-multiplexing）实现并行非局域纠缠门。通过绝热条件抑制门不保真度和串扰误差，并对阱频慢漂移（离子阱的主要误差源之一）具有鲁棒性。同时优化量子比特映射和频率带分配以降低激光功率需求并提升并行度。

Result: 针对512离子二维晶体上的[[248,10,18]]二元循环码数值示例，优化后中等激光功率即可实现并行操作；在现实噪声参数下逻辑错误率可达10^-12。

Conclusion: 频率复用方案为在大规模二维离子晶体中高效执行qLDPC码的并行非局域综合征测量提供了一条可行路径，兼具可扩展性、对阱频漂移的鲁棒性和较低的硬件资源需求。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes admit high encoding rates but require nonlocal entangling gates for syndrome measurement. Instead of physically moving the qubits which slows down with the increasing qubit number, here we propose to achieve parallel nonlocal entangling gates on a two-dimensional (2D) ion crystal using frequency-multiplexing. Adiabatic conditions ensure the suppression of gate infidelity and crosstalk error, as well as their robustness against slow drift in the trap frequency which is a leading error source in ion trap. We consider a numerical example of a $[[248,10,18]]$ bivariate bicycle code on a 2D crystal of 512 ions. By optimizing the mapping of the qubits and the assignment of the frequency bands for multiplexing, we show that a moderate laser power is sufficient for parallelism, and that a logical error rate of $10^{-12}$ can be achieved under realistic noise parameters.

</details>


### [116] [Gravitationally Induced Entanglement Across an Event Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04104)
*Hatim Salih*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在引力推迟势模型中证明了粒子越过黑洞事件视界后仍能与外部粒子动态纠缠，但径向提取该纠缠态需非惯性减速，触发软引力子轫致辐射，产生退相干下界Γ≥729/160πΦ；而宏观光学质量可通过量子擦除实现切向纠缠收割，揭示了时空在径向不可逆地破坏纠缠、切向却允许纠缠态横向遥传至无穷的几何对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来学界假定粒子一旦不带纠缠地跨越黑洞事件视界，便无法再与外部粒子动态纠缠。该工作挑战这一传统认知，探究在引力背景下跨事件视界纠缠生成的物理可能性及其观测后果。

Method: 采用引力推迟势模型，分析自由下落的径向空间叠加态跨越事件视界时的纠缠生成动力学；进而考察径向提取该纠缠态所需的非惯性减速过程，并借助软引力子轫致辐射理论推导退相界；另通过宏观光学质量的量子擦除方案实现切向纠缠收割，对比两种几何路径下的纠缠行为。

Result: 证明了跨事件视界的纠缠可以从零开始生成，但径向提取受制于严格的退相下界Γ≥729/160πΦ，该退相干由软引力子轫致辐射引起；而切向路径通过量子擦除可成功获取纠缠，无需付出退相干代价。

Conclusion: 揭示了一种引人注目的几何对偶性：时空在沿径向使局域化质量被拖离视界之际不可逆地破坏纠缠，同时却允许纠缠态沿横截方向（切向）通过遥传方式到达无穷远。

Abstract: It has long been assumed that a particle---having crossed a black hole event horizon unentangled with another particle in the exterior universe---can no longer dynamically entangle with that particle. Here, we demonstrate within a gravitational retarded-potential model that entanglement can in fact be created from scratch between freely falling spatial superpositions across an event horizon. Yet, extracting this state radially requires non-inertial deceleration, triggering soft-graviton bremsstrahlung, and establishing a strict dephasing bound in terms of entangling phase, $Γ\ge\frac{729}{160π}Φ$. This decoheres the entangled state. By contrast, equivalent macroscopic optical masses allow local tangential harvesting of entanglement via quantum erasure, thus revealing a remarkable geometric duality: Spacetime irreversibly degrades entanglement the moment the localized mass is dragged away from the horizon, while allowing the transverse teleportation of its entangled state to infinity.

</details>


### [117] [Non-local Magic: closed-form solution and equivalence with magic of purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04119)
*Michele Viscardi,Lorenzo Leone,Alioscia Hamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双量子比特纯态非局域魔力的精确解析刻画。作者证明了对数稳定子保真度，非局域魔力的优化存在仅依赖Schmidt谱的闭合解析解；并引入"纯化魔力"作为混合态资源理论，证明其自由态为归一化稳定子码投影算子，且非局域魔力等于约化密度算子酉轨道上最小纯化魔力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域魔力虽定义自然（度量双部分量子态中无法通过局域酉变换去除的非稳定子性质），但其计算通常涉及对局域酉的困难优化。作者旨在寻找精确解析刻画，并为非局域魔力提供混合态资源理论解释。

Method: 证明了对于对数稳定子保真度，非局域魔力优化可化归为仅依赖Schmidt谱的闭合解析形式。定义了"纯化魔力"（混合态所有纯化中纯态魔力的最小值），以此诱导资源理论，其自由态恰为归一化稳定子码投影算子。对于对数稳定子保真度，纯化魔力可用Uhlmann保真度给出基于距离的表达。最后证明非局域魔力与约化密度算子酉轨道上纯化魔力的最小值精确相等。

Result: 给出了非局域魔力（针对对数稳定子保真度）的闭合解析解；建立了纯化魔力的资源理论框架；证明了非局域魔力与酉轨道上最小纯化魔力的精确等价关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为非局域魔力提供了高效的解析刻画，并赋予其混合态资源理论的物理诠释，同时揭示了纯化与非局域魔力之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Non-local magic quantifies the non-stabilizerness of a bipartite quantum state that cannot be removed by local unitary transformations. Despite its natural definition, its evaluation generally requires a difficult optimization over local unitaries. Here, we show that for the log-stabilizer fidelity this optimization admits an exact closed-form analytic solution depending only on the Schmidt spectrum. We then introduce the magic of purification, defined as the minimum pure-state magic over all purifications of a mixed state, and show that it naturally induces a resource theory whose free states are normalized stabilizer-code projectors. For the log-stabilizer fidelity, the magic of purification admits a distance-based formulation in terms of the Uhlmann fidelity. Remarkably, we prove that non-local magic coincides with the minimum magic of purification along the unitary orbit of the reduced density operator. Our results provide both an efficient analytical characterization and a mixed-state resource-theoretic interpretation of non-local magic.

</details>


### [118] [Characterizing Large Scale Quantum Systems with Error Per Circuit Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04132)
*Travis Hurant,Arian Vezvaee,Swarnadeep Majumder,Aniket Dalvi,Jude Alnas,Kenneth R. Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出EPCL（每电路层误差）基准，一种基于重叠测量的电路级量子性能评测方法。该方法通过对两个不相交量子寄存器施加相同随机电路并测量输出态间重叠随电路深度的衰减，来估计有效层极化参数。EPCL无需经典模拟理想输出分布、无需恢复到已知参考态，且兼容包括非Clifford门在内的任意门集。作者推导了集成平均去极化模型下的期望重叠衰减，并通过数值模拟和IBM量子硬件实验（8/16比特）验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路级量子基准测试存在局限：有些需要结构化门集和昂贵的编译过程（如Clifford门），有些需要经典模拟参考输出来验证理想态，还有些依赖无法捕捉全寄存器行为的子系统分解。这些限制使得基准测试难以广泛应用，无法全面反映噪声在相互作用操作中的累积效应。因此需要一种不依赖经典模拟、不限制门集、能捕捉全寄存器行为的电路级基准方法。

Method: EPCL的核心方法是：将相同随机电路分别施加到两个不相交的量子寄存器上，测量两个输出态之间的重叠（overlap）作为电路深度d的函数。随深度增加，噪声导致的量子态偏离使重叠按指数衰减。作者在集成平均去极化模型下推导了期望衰减形式，通过拟合衰减参数来估计有效层极化。该方法不需经典模拟理想输出分布，不需恢复到已知参考态，兼容任意门集（包括非Clifford门）。数值模拟研究了弱局部随机噪声和较强随机噪声下的行为，并分析了相干误差和寄存器间相关性对衰减的影响。

Result: 数值模拟表明EPCL在弱局部随机噪声下能准确恢复预测的极化参数，在较强随机噪声下仍保持单指数衰减的良好描述。固定纠缠层中的相干误差需要通过Pauli twirling或随机编译来产生期望的衰减；寄存器间相关性会引入额外的协方差项。IBM量子硬件上的8比特和16比特实现展示了清晰的EPCL衰减曲线，证明了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: EPCL作为一种测度聚合寄存器性能的电路级量子基准方法，不需要经典模拟理想电路输出，也不受限于结构化门集。它通过双寄存器重叠测量提供了一种可扩展、硬件友好且理论上有明确解释的基准测试方案，对评估和比较不同量子系统具有实际价值。

Abstract: Quantum benchmarks provide compact measures of performance that are important for evaluating and comparing quantum systems. Circuit-level benchmarks are particularly valuable because they capture the accumulated effects of noise across interacting operations, but existing approaches may require structured gate sets and costly compilation, classical simulation of reference outputs, or subsystem decompositions that do not capture full-register behavior. We introduce Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL), an overlap-based circuit-level benchmark that estimates an effective layer polarization by applying identical random circuits to two disjoint quantum registers and measuring the overlap between their output states as a function of circuit depth. EPCL avoids classical simulation of ideal output distributions and recovery to a known reference state, and is compatible with arbitrary gate sets, including non-Clifford gates.
  We derive the expected overlap decay under an ensemble-averaged depolarizing model and identify the assumptions under which the fitted decay parameter represents an effective layer polarization. Numerical simulations show that EPCL recovers the predicted polarization under weak local stochastic noise and remains well described by a single-exponential decay at stronger stochastic noise levels. The simulations further show that coherent errors associated with fixed entangling layers may require Pauli twirling or randomized compiling to produce the expected decay, while inter-register correlations contribute an additional covariance term to the measured overlap. Finally, experiments on IBM quantum hardware demonstrate clear EPCL decay in 8- and 16-qubit implementations. These results support EPCL as a method for measuring aggregate register performance without requiring classical simulation of ideal circuit outputs or restriction to structured gate sets.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient Conversion of Optical to Mechanical States Close to the Single-Quantum Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04150)
*Alexander Rolf Korsch,Liu Chen,Pedro V. Pinho,Boris Müllendorff,Jan N. Kirchhof,Yong Yu,Thiago P. Mayer Alegre,Simon Gröblacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在毫开尔文温度下运行的腔光机械晶体中，实现了接近单量子极限的低噪声光子-声子态转移，达到0.76的创纪录转换效率、7.3微秒的机械存储寿命和超过4.5 MHz的可调转换带宽，并通过HBT测量证实了转换声子态的相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的OMIT转换协议受限于室温运行产生的热机械噪声，只能处理具有大相干态光子数的经典输入信号。为了推进光机系统在混合量子信息处理和声子态工程中的应用，有必要在接近单量子水平实现低噪声的光子-声子相干转换。

Method: 在毫开尔文温度下运行光机械晶体（optomechanical crystal），使用弱相干光脉冲（少光子水平）作为输入，实现低噪声的光子-声子态转换。通过设计低温环境来抑制热机械噪声，并利用Hanbury Brown-Twiss（HBT）测量表征检索信号的相干性质。

Result: 实现了光子-声子转换效率η=0.76（创纪录水平）、机械存储寿命T₁=7.3微秒、可调转换带宽超过4.5 MHz。HBT测量证实了转换声子态的相干性，并量化了转换过程中的低附加热噪声（n_th=9.0）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了光机械晶体作为GHz机械模式的高效光学接口的有效性，为确定性单量子水平机械态制备和混合量子信息处理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Coherent interfaces between optical photons and mechanical excitations provide a promising route towards phonon-state engineering and hybrid quantum information processing. Cavity optomechanical systems enable such interfaces via optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT), allowing coherent mapping between traveling optical fields and localized mechanical modes. However, previous implementations of OMIT conversion protocols were limited to classical input signals with large coherent state photon numbers due to room temperature operation and corresponding thermal mechanical noise. Here, we demonstrate efficient low-noise photon-phonon state transfer close to the single-quantum regime in an optomechanical crystal operated at Millikelvin temperatures. Using weak coherent optical input pulses at the few-photon level, we achieve a record-level photon-phonon conversion efficiency of $η=0.76$, a mechanical storage lifetime of $T_\mathrm{1}=7.3~μs$, and a tunable conversion bandwidth exceeding 4.5 MHz. Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurements of the retrieved signal demonstrate the coherent nature of the converted phononic state, evidencing low added thermal noise in the conversion process ($n_\mathrm{th}=9.0$). These results establish optomechanical crystals as efficient optical interfaces to GHz mechanical modes and provide a pathway toward deterministic single-quantum-level mechanical state preparation.

</details>


### [120] [Spurious quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04157)
*Shashaank Khanna,Matthew F. Pusey,Roger Colbeck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了"虚假量子关联"（spurious quantum correlations）现象：某些关联在一种因果结构中看似是非经典的量子关联，但在另一种因果结构中却可以无需精细调谐地获得经典解释。文章还区分了那些确实无法在任何替代因果结构中被经典解释的真正非经典量子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell定理表明贝尔因果结构中存在无法被经典解释的量子关联，但替代因果结构（如允许超光速影响的结构）可以提供经典解释，不过这些解释通常需要精细调谐。作者希望更深入地理解"量子关联是否可被经典解释"这一问题在不同因果结构之间的相对性，并识别出真正本质非经典与仅相对非经典的关联之间的区别。

Method: 本文通过对比不同因果结构下量子关联的可解释性，考察是否存在这样的因果结构：（1）在该结构中显现非经典量子关联；（2）同样的关联在另一种因果结构中无需精细调谐即可获得经典解释；（3）第二种结构不违反相对论所隐含的因果结构自然约束。作者据此分类并分析量子关联的本质。

Result: 研究发现存在"虚假量子关联"：某些因果结构支持的非经典量子关联，在实际上的另一种因果结构（且不违反相对论自然约束）中可以获得无精细调谐的经典解释。然而，与贝尔因果结构中的非经典量子关联类似，也存在一些因果结构中的非经典量子关联无法在任何替代因果结构中无需精细调谐地获得经典解释——这些才是真正本质非经典的量子关联。

Conclusion: 量子关联的非经典性并非绝对的属性，而是相对于所假定的因果结构而言的。存在"虚假量子关联"使其在一种因果结构中看似非经典，但在另一种合理的因果结构中可被经典解释。但同时也存在在某些因果结构中真正不可归约的非经典量子关联，它们与贝尔场景一样，在任何无精细调谐的经典因果解释下都不可还原。

Abstract: In his seminal paper, Bell [Physics Physique Fizika 1, 195 (1964)] considers the correlations that result from space-like separated measurements on a pair of entangled particles. He uses relativity theory to motivate the Bell causal structure, then shows the existence of quantum correlations that cannot be explained classically within this causal structure. Classical explanations of such quantum correlations are possible in alternative causal structures, for instance, those that allow superluminal causal influences, but, as shown in [New Journal of Physics 17 033002 (2015)], all such alternative explanations require fine tuning (causation without correlation). Here we discuss the existence of spurious quantum correlations --- correlations that look quantum in one causal structure, but have a natural classical explanation in another. More precisely, there are causal structures that admit non-classical quantum correlations, but for which the same correlations have a classical explanation in another causal structure without fine tuning. The realisation in the other causal structure can also be achieved without breaking any natural constraints on the causal structure that follow from relativity theory. However, similarly to non-classical quantum correlations in the Bell causal structure, we find other causal structures with non-classical quantum correlations that do not have a classical causal explanation in any alternative causal structure without fine tuning.

</details>


### [121] [Vanilla Exact Synthesis of CNOT Circuits is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04160)
*Chenjian Li,Ji Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了最基础形式的精确CNOT综合问题（无辅助比特、全互联、固定标号量子比特、恒等输入）的决定性版本是NP完全的，优化版本是NP困难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多种相关的CNOT综合模型已被证明是计算困难的，但既有证明都依赖额外结构（如受限量子比特连通性、编码输入或不受限的中间变量）。最基本的设定——恒等输入、固定数量的标号量子比特、无辅助比特、全互联CNOT——的复杂度长期悬而未决，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 通过两步多项式归约证明：先从网格图上的哈密顿路径问题出发，利用一元编码映射将网格图等距嵌入超立方体；随后将该超立方体哈密顿路径问题编码为基本CNOT综合问题。关键创新在于引入额外的"记录器"量子比特，将所需的中间顶点访问信息编码到最终变换中，从而强制任何CNOT电路实现必须符合预期的路径结构——因为CNOT综合本身只指定最终奇偶矩阵，无法直接约束中间步骤。

Result: 证明了vanilla精确CNOT综合的决定性问题为NP完全，优化问题为NP困难。该结果还直接蕴含多个相关问题的困难性，包括GL(n,2)上的最短词问题、GL(n,2)上Cayley图的距离计算、顺序XOR程序的最小化以及相位多项式电路的精确综合。

Conclusion: 本文通过精巧的记录器量子比特构造，解决了CNOT综合无法直接编码中间过程的根本障碍，确立了最基础CNOT综合问题的计算复杂度下界，并为一系列相关综合问题提供了统一的困难性证明。

Abstract: Exact CNOT synthesis asks for a minimum-size CNOT circuit implementing an invertible linear transformation. Although several related synthesis models have been shown to be computationally hard, their hardness proofs rely on additional structure such as restricted qubit connectivity, encoded inputs, or unrestricted intermediate variables. The complexity of the most basic setting---identity input, a fixed number of labelled qubits, no ancillas, and all-to-all CNOT connectivity---had remained unresolved. In this work, we prove that the decision version of this vanilla exact CNOT synthesis problem is NP-complete, and consequently that its optimization version is NP-hard. Our proof gives a polynomial-time reduction from the Hamiltonian-path problem on grid graphs in two steps. First, we isometrically embed the grid graph into a hypercube via a unary encoding map. We then encode this hypercube Hamiltonian path problem into vanilla exact CNOT synthesis. The main challenge is that CNOT synthesis specifies only the final parity matrix and cannot directly enforce the intermediate vertex visits required by a Hamiltonian path. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce extra recorder qubits that encode the required intermediate vertex visits into the final transformation, forcing any CNOT circuit implementation to realize the intended path structure. Beyond CNOT synthesis, our result directly implies hardness for several related problems, including the shortest word problem over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, distance computation on Cayley graphs over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, minimization of sequential XOR programs, and exact synthesis of phase polynomial circuits.

</details>


### [122] [Parameterised graph theory for tensor networks: entanglement rerouting, structural simplification, and agnostic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04165)
*Matthias C. Caro,Natalie McHugh,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用参数化图论研究张量网络态（TNS）的表示与层析问题，证明cutwidth和tree-cutwidth约束了TNS转化为MPS/TTN所需的键维数开销，并推导出依赖图参数的样本与计算复杂度上界，同时将TNS学习框架扩展到任意已知图上的非实然（agnostic）场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然参数化图论已被证明对分析张量网络模拟有用，但其对张量网络表示和层析的启示尚不清楚，特别是哪些图参数决定TNS能否以可处理的MPS或TTN形式表示，以及哪些参数控制学习该状态的复杂度，这些问题尚未得到回答。

Method: 利用参数化图论工具：首先证明了cutwidth和tree-cutwidth限制TNS表示为MPS/TTN所需的键维数开销，其证明基于"纠缠重路由"（entanglement rerouting）这一思想，即经典网络中信息路由的张量网络类比；其次通过扩展Cramer等人（2010）的去纠缠MPS学习器到TTN和任意已知图上的张量网络，推导出样本与计算复杂度的图上界，引入新参数"学习复杂度"（learning complexity）；最后将框架扩展到非实然设置，提出agnostic学习器，输出达到最优张量网络态ε加性误差范围内的纯态。

Result: 建立了cutwidth/tree-cutwidth与TNS压缩为MPS或TTN所需的键维数开销之间的联系；推导出依赖cutwidth、tree-cutwidth和学习复杂度等图参数的层析样本与计算复杂度上界；为任意输入态（不限实然设定）提供了具有明确图相关复杂度界的agnostic学习器。

Conclusion: 参数化图论为张量网络态的紧凑表示和可学习性提供了系统性的理论框架，揭示了图结构参数（cutwidth、tree-cutwidth等）在决定TNS计算复杂性中的核心作用，并成功将学习器从MPS推广到任意图结构的TNS与非实然学习场景。

Abstract: Parameterised graph theory studies how the complexity of graph-theoretic problems depends on structural parameters of the input graph. This perspective has proved useful in analysing tensor-network simulation (Markov and Shi, 2008). Its implications for tensor-network representations and tomography are less well understood. In particular, which graph parameters determine whether a tensor-network state (TNS) admits a tractable matrix product state (MPS) or tree tensor network (TTN) representation, and which control the complexity of learning the state?
  We address these questions using parameterised graph theory. First, we show that cutwidth and tree-cutwidth bound the bond dimension overhead required to represent a TNS as an MPS or TTN. In the TTN case, tree-cutwidth also bounds the local dimension of the grouped subsystems. The proofs are based on entanglement rerouting, a tensor-network analogue of rerouting information in a classical network. Second, we derive graph-dependent upper bounds on the sample and computational complexity of realisable TNS tomography, with exponents that depend on cutwidth, tree-cutwidth, and a new graph parameter, learning complexity, which we bound in terms of degree and treewidth. We obtain these results by extending the disentangling MPS learner of (Cramer et al., 2010), as analysed further in (Bakshi et al., 2025; Lin et al., 2025), to TTNs and to tensor networks on arbitrary known graphs. Finally, we extend the framework beyond the realisable setting. For an arbitrary input state, our agnostic learner outputs a pure state whose fidelity is within additive error $ε$ of the optimum over tensor-network states on the given graph with a given bond dimension, with explicit graph-dependent bounds on sample and computational complexity.

</details>


### [123] [Measurements on the separated subsystems of an entangled state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04178)
*Gregory D. Scholes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了复合量子系统中分离子系统的测量问题，提出可以通过自由向量空间中的陪集来解析一般纠缠态在分离子系统上的投影态，从而无需引入随机坍缩假设，并从该视角解释了非局域性的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合量子系统的纠缠态已被广泛研究，但当系统被物理分离后，对分离子系统A和B进行测量时，如何从张量积基下给出的纠缠态中解析出每个分离子系统的相关量子态一直不清楚（仅可分态的情况是明确的）。这一方法论空白阻碍了对分离子系统中测量本质的理解。

Method: 作者提出，任何一般纠缠态在分离子系统测量下所检测到的投影，可以通过考虑定义张量积空间的自由向量空间中的（对应陪集）态来获得。该方法不依赖于随机坍缩假设，而是基于自由向量空间的结构来解析分离子系统上的投影。

Result: 证明了从自由向量空间的陪集视角处理分离子系统时，可以一致地获得纠缠态的局部投影结果，并且无需引入随机坍缩机制。非局域性被解释为分离子系统测量方式对所投射的可能测量结果造成的效应。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种无需随机坍缩假设即可描述分离子系统中测量态的新框架，将非局域性重新理解为分离测量投影方式的自然结果，为进一步理解量子非局域性的本质提供了新的数学基础。

Abstract: The entangled states of composite quantum systems are well studied. The particle-like nature of these systems also means that, while entangled, they can be physically separated and measurements performed on the separated subsystems. Measurements on the separated subsystems A and B should pertain to vectors in the local Hilbert space of each subsystem, but to date it has not been clear how to elucidate the relevant states of the separated subsystems because it is not obvious how to resolve them from states given in the tensor product basis, except for the separable states. Here it is shown that the projections of any general entangled state that are detected by measurements on the separated subsystems can be obtained considering the corresponding (cosets of) states in the free vector space from which the tensor product space is defined. The result eliminates the need to invoke random collapse, and from this perspective nonlocality arises because of the way measurements on each separated subsystem projects possible measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum communication and Bell nonlocality require infinite classical communication to simulate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04182)
*Carlos de Gois,Thyago S. R. Santos,Carlos Vieira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了任意量子维度下经典通信模拟量子通信与量子关联的问题，发现维度4及以上无法用有限经典通信精确模拟，并给出了维度3（qutrit）用357个经典比特精确模拟的显式协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统虽然可以制备在连续统状态中，但其传输经典信息的能力有限背景与量子关联超过经典关联但不传信息的特点暗示可用有限经典通信模拟量子统计。此前仅解决了最小非平凡维度（qubit，2个经典比特），更高维度问题悬而未决数十年。

Method: 针对所有量子维度分别处理两个问题：qutrit通信与双qutrit纠缠关联的精确模拟采用显式构造的经典通信协议；维度4及以上采用不可能性证明，表明即使有无限共享随机性，有限经典通信也无法精确模拟ququart通信及双ququart纠缠关联。

Result: 维度4开始出现意外的定性转变：无有限量经典通信能精确模拟ququart通信或两个纠缠ququart的所有量子关联。此前人们可能预期若有转变会出现在qutrit（维度3）层面；相反，本文构造了用357个经典比特精确模拟qutrit通信的显式协议，从而也模拟了两个纠缠qutrit的所有关联。

Conclusion: 量子统计可被经典通信模拟的边界并非在最小的非平凡维度3处，而是在维度4处发生定性断裂。qutrit情形可用有限经典资源完全模拟，而从ququart开始模拟变得不可能，揭示了量子与经典模拟能力的深层结构。

Abstract: A quantum system of any fixed dimension can be prepared in a continuum of states, yet it cannot be used to transmit an unlimited amount of classical information. Similarly, the correlations observed between measurement outcomes on separate parts of a shared quantum system can be stronger than classical correlations, but they cannot transmit information. These fundamental limitations suggest that the statistics observed from quantum communication and quantum correlations may admit a simulation using a finite amount of classical communication. This expectation is confirmed in the smallest nontrivial quantum dimension, with two classical bits being necessary and sufficient to exactly simulate qubit communication and all correlations between qubits. Despite significant efforts during the previous decades, this remained the only solved case. Here we resolve both problems for every quantum dimension. The solution reveals an unexpected qualitative transition starting at dimension four: no finite amount of classical communication can exactly simulate ququart communication nor all quantum correlations of two entangled ququarts, even with unlimited shared randomness. One might have expected this transition, if it existed, to appear already for qutrits. Instead, we construct an explicit protocol that exactly simulates qutrit communication using $357$ classical bits, and consequently, all correlations of two entangled qutrits.

</details>
